## Best date plans

### 2026-09-17 — Red Hook Tavern + FUMBL Fest

**Dinner, then FUMBL Fest.** American tavern at Red Hook Tavern. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. FUMBL Fest starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=329+Van+Brunt+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=290+Conover+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://ticketstripe.com/fumblfest) · [Restaurant](https://www.redhooktavern.com/)

**Dinner, then The Hunger Games: Catching Fire Entirely From Memory.** Pasta and Italian-American at Pasta Louise. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. The Hunger Games: Catching Fire Entirely From Memory starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.

## Where to eat

A tightened NYC dining edit for 2026-09-17, keeping sourced set menus, dated events, current restaurant reports and verified time-window offers while stripping unsupported prices and heatmap-style framing.

Dining research: 2026-09-17T10:39:55+00:00

No Michelin recognition claims were retained or added. Restaurant self-descriptions/images mentioning stars were treated as unverified because no official Michelin Guide restaurant pages were checked in this pass.

### Manhatta dining room prix-fixe menus

Special menus — Manhatta

High-floor dining room offers set-price dinner and weekend lunch formats, useful for a planned splurge with clear menu pricing.

Price: Dinner: three courses $120, wine pairing $75; four courses $148, wine pairing $95. Weekend lunch: two courses $65, three courses $78  
Schedule: Dinner and weekend lunch; exact service hours not captured in checked excerpt  
Conditions: Reservation needs, tax, tip, and service charge not stated in checked excerpt. Source: https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/)

### Crown Shy lunch prix-fixe mention

Special menus — Crown Shy

Crown Shy’s official lunch menu states that a prix-fixe menu is available alongside a la carte lunch items, giving a set-menu option downtown.

Price: Prix-fixe price unknown; a la carte lunch items shown at $8-$49  
Schedule: Lunch; exact lunch days/hours not captured in checked excerpt  
Conditions: Official menu text says a lunch prix-fixe menu is available, but the accessible excerpt did not state its price, course count, service days/hours, tax, tip or service charge. Source: https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Journey Through India tasting menu

Special menus — Junoon

Junoon’s official menu lists a structured regional tasting menu with whole-table participation, useful for a modern Indian fine-dining set menu.

Price: $165 per person; optional $90 wine pairing  
Schedule: Excluding Sundays  
Conditions: Whole table participation required; excluding Sundays. Official menu states a 20% service charge will be added to parties of 5 or more. Other tax, tip, and reservation requirements not stated in checked excerpt. Source: https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Chef’s Choice Menu at The Musket Room

## Discovery coverage

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: Green-Wood 19/39; Brooklyn 23/39. Largest source exceeds target because Green-Wood published a dense, fully verified in-window festival while many mandatory calendars were blocked or unreadable; Brooklyn target met.
- **[arts] BAM — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted calendar anchor; fetched visible content was an out-of-window Halloween family listing, no retained arts-lane events.
- **[arts] Broadway.org — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted shows and performance-times pages; fetched content did not expose retainable show-specific listings beyond general notices.
- **[arts] Brooklyn Magazine — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted homepage; fetched content exposed subscription prompt and no usable in-window event listings.
- **[arts] Brooklyn Museum — blocked (fetch failed):** Calendar returned HTTP 429 rate limit to fetcher.
- **[arts] Bryant Park — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted activities/programs page; visible categories did not yield arts-lane in-window retained items.
- **[arts] Comedy Cellar — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted reservations page; fetched content showed policies but no dated public lineup to retain.
- **[arts] Daily coverage — used:** 2026-09-17: 11; 2026-09-18: 8; 2026-09-19: 9; 2026-09-20: 10; 2026-09-21: 0; 2026-09-22: 1; 2026-09-23: 0.
